# MANIFEST_LOCAL.md — what was copied into this folder, from where, and what was not

**This file is a record of history, not a dependency.** The laptop paths below say where each copy came from on
2026-10-08. Nothing in this folder reads them; every live reference is a grid path (`MANIFEST_GRID.md`) or a repository
and commit (`MANIFEST_CODE.md`). This is the only file in the folder that names a laptop path.

Built 2026-10-08 by the copier recorded in the build log (1,239 files, 63,636,987 bytes copied; one PDF removed afterwards,
section 2, leaving 1,238 files and 61,541,541 bytes; 1,265 source files not copied, grouped in Appendix B). Disposition rows: the 91 rows with `project_folder = 06_transitional_ct_census` in
`../_plan/DISPOSITION.tsv` (36 copy, 55 reference, 0 excluded). Rules applied: no imaging data, weights, archives,
`.git`, caches, logs over 2 MB, files over 25 MB (none met in this folder's rows), credential-like names, personal or
administrative material; links never followed. Where a row named a folder, its text files were copied and its renders
left by path, as the row says.

## Final pass of 2026-10-08 (after the critic's check of the whole tree)

Rows added by the final pass; the sections above are the first build and the verifier's fix pass as they were. Sources are laptop paths of 2026-10-08 (history, not dependencies). sha1 is the first 16 hex digits of the copy.

### Salvaged: the AACA 2026 LSTV fusion pipeline (`code/aaca_lstv_mri/lstv_fusion/`, 9 files)

The critic found that the code behind `results/aaca_lstv_mri/lstv_cohort_summary.csv` and `lstv_per_study.csv` was in no project folder and in no manifest: inventory 3 had lumped the laptop folder `nsurg/AACA/fm/` into one row with the lstv-annotation-tool working copies and written 'the GitHub repo is the record', but lstv-fusion is a different package. It does have a repository: https://github.com/Gregory-Schwing-MD-PhD/lstv-fusion, branch `main`, one commit 3c8f5c1 ('first commit', 2026-02-16; `git log` of the laptop clone, 2026-10-08); whether that remote exists and is reachable was not checked (no network in this pass). The tracked files are copied; `.git/`, the empty `logs/`, `models/` and `output/` folders and the untracked `.gitignore` are not. `MANIFEST_CODE.md` section 6b records the repository.
